<main class="wrap stories"><article class="story top" id="story-50378228" data-id="50378228" data-tags="Security" data-score="2978" data-comments="747" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50378228" data-time="1793881142">
<a class="thumb" href="https://sshhole.com" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://sshhole.com/receipts/3bsd-passwd.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='sshhole.com' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://sshhole.com" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>sshole</span><span class='zh'>sshole</span></a><span class="dom">sshhole.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenSSH on musl Linux has reportedly allowed access to passwordless accounts since 2016.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，musl Linux 上的 OpenSSH 自 2016 年起可能允许进入无密码账户。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenSSH on musl Linux has reportedly allowed access to passwordless accounts since 2016. The issue relates to how Unix historically represented accounts that should never permit login, including accounts marked with an asterisk in the password file. The behavior can therefore expose accounts that were intended to be inaccessible.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，musl Linux 上的 OpenSSH 自 2016 年起可能允许进入无密码账户。问题与 Unix 处理本应禁止登录的账户的历史方式有关，包括在密码文件中用星号标记的账户。因此，原本应不可访问的账户可能被暴露。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/05/top_stories_11052026'><span class='en'>Nov 5</span><span class='zh'>11月5日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>2978</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50378228' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>747</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50385272" data-id="50385272" data-tags="AI|Privacy|Security|Code" data-score="1605" data-comments="533" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50385272" data-time="1793917035">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/107757" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">02</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/107757" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Code sends your email address to websites</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 会将你的邮箱地址发送给网站</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A reported Claude Code bug exposed a user&#x27;s email address to arbitrary third parties in the User-Agent header during web searches.</span><span class='zh'>有用户报告称，Claude Code 在进行网页搜索时，会通过 User-Agent 请求头将其邮箱地址暴露给任意第三方。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A reported Claude Code bug exposed a user&#x27;s email address to arbitrary third parties in the User-Agent header during web searches. The user says this happened without permission and was followed by a large increase in scam emails. They argue that the behavior should be removed rather than controlled by an opt-in or opt-out setting.</span><span class='zh'>有用户报告称，Claude Code 在进行网页搜索时，会通过 User-Agent 请求头将其邮箱地址暴露给任意第三方。该用户表示，未经允许发生这一行为后，自己收到的诈骗邮件大幅增加。用户认为，这种行为不应通过加入或退出选项控制，而应从系统中彻底移除。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/05/top_stories_11052026'><span class='en'>Nov 5</span><span class='zh'>11月5日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>1605</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50385272' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>533</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50364336" data-id="50364336" data-tags="AI|Security" data-score="1271" data-comments="743" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50364336" data-time="1793793137">
<a class="thumb" href="https://spectrum.ieee.org/ai-in-cybersecurity-php2shell-vulnerability" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=68544156&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C312%2C0%2C313' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='spectrum.ieee.org' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">03</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://spectrum.ieee.org/ai-in-cybersecurity-php2shell-vulnerability" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI is forcing us to pay off 40 years of technical debt</span><span class='zh'>AI 正迫使我们偿还 40 年积累的技术债</span></a><span class="dom">spectrum.ieee.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI is accelerating both attacks and defenses in cybersecurity.</span><span class='zh'>AI 正在同时加速网络攻击和防御。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI is accelerating both attacks and defenses in cybersecurity. That acceleration is exposing painful technical debt in fragile legacy systems built up over roughly four decades. The pressure from AI is therefore forcing organizations to address weaknesses that older systems had left unresolved.</span><span class='zh'>AI 正在同时加速网络攻击和防御。这样的加速暴露了脆弱遗留系统中积累约 40 年的严重技术债。AI 带来的压力因此迫使组织处理旧系统长期遗留下来的弱点。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/04/top_stories_11042026'><span class='en'>Nov 4</span><span class='zh'>11月4日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>1271</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50364336' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>743</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50371246" data-id="50371246" data-tags="Security|Privacy|Politics" data-score="1249" data-comments="431" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50371246" data-time="1793825118">
<a class="thumb" href="https://layer3intel.com/blog/residential-proxies-white-house-network" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://layer3intel.com/eop_proxy_activity.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='layer3intel.com' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">04</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://layer3intel.com/blog/residential-proxies-white-house-network" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Residential proxies detected on White House network</span><span class='zh'>白宫网络被发现使用住宅代理</span></a><span class="dom">layer3intel.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Residential proxy traffic exited the White House network 236 times over 30 days, passing through 28 public proxy services.</span><span class='zh'>过去 30 天内，有住宅代理流量 236 次从白宫网络流出，并经过 28 个公共代理服务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Residential proxy traffic exited the White House network 236 times over 30 days, passing through 28 public proxy services. The network, AS2650, is registered to the Executive Office of the President, which hosts the White House network. Residential proxies route customers’ traffic through everyday devices so it appears to originate from those devices’ networks.</span><span class='zh'>过去 30 天内，有住宅代理流量 236 次从白宫网络流出，并经过 28 个公共代理服务。该网络的 AS2650 注册于负责托管白宫网络的美国总统行政办公室。住宅代理会让客户的流量经由普通设备转发，使其看起来来自这些设备所在的网络。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Politics' style='--c:#f97316'>Politics</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/04/top_stories_11042026'><span class='en'>Nov 4</span><span class='zh'>11月4日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>1249</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50371246' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>431</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50424581" data-id="50424581" data-tags="Education|Entertainment|Data" data-score="1246" data-comments="167" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50424581" data-time="1794226762">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.foldableflight.com" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://static.wixstatic.com/media/0641fb_4ff6a49def494e379b4f3b585c4ac4a8%7Emv2_d_2482_1412_s_2.jpg/v1/fit/w_2500,h_1330,al_c/0641fb_4ff6a49def494e379b4f3b585c4ac4a8%7Emv2_d_2482_1412_s_2.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='foldableflight.com' data-c='#60a5fa' data-tag='Education'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">05</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.foldableflight.com" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Foldable Flight: The Best Paper Airplanes</span><span class='zh'>折叠飞行：最佳纸飞机</span></a><span class="dom">foldableflight.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The site presents a large database of paper airplanes with step-by-step video folding tutorials.</span><span class='zh'>该网站提供大型纸飞机数据库，并为每种机型配备分步折叠视频教程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The site presents a large database of paper airplanes with step-by-step video folding tutorials. One World Record prototype is reported to fly more than 300 feet. The Unite model holds a world record for time aloft at 31.2 seconds.</span><span class='zh'>该网站提供大型纸飞机数据库，并为每种机型配备分步折叠视频教程。据称，一款世界纪录原型机可以飞行超过 300 英尺。Unite 机型则以 31.2 秒的滞空时间保持世界纪录。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Education' style='--c:#60a5fa'>Education</span><span class='tag' data-t='Entertainment' style='--c:#c084fc'>Entertainment</span><span class='tag' data-t='Data' style='--c:#6366f1'>Data</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/09/top_stories_11092026'><span class='en'>Nov 9</span><span class='zh'>11月9日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>1246</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50424581' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>167</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50441707" data-id="50441707" data-tags="Privacy|Hardware" data-score="1142" data-comments="432" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50441707" data-time="1794324320">
<a class="thumb" href="https://docodethatmatters.com/home-automation-overnight-success/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://docodethatmatters.com/images/home-automation/t1-07.webp' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='docodethatmatters.com' data-c='#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">06</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://docodethatmatters.com/home-automation-overnight-success/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Smart home devices should work without the internet</span><span class='zh'>智能家居应当无需互联网也能运行</span></a><span class="dom">docodethatmatters.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author describes a decade-long home-automation rule: no core function in the house should depend on a vendor’s server.</span><span class='zh'>作者介绍了坚持十年的家庭自动化原则：家中的核心功能不应依赖厂商服务器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author describes a decade-long home-automation rule: no core function in the house should depend on a vendor’s server. As a result, the home continues operating when the internet goes down. The post presents the other automation rules and two related talks that grew out of this principle.</span><span class='zh'>作者介绍了坚持十年的家庭自动化原则：家中的核心功能不应依赖厂商服务器。因此，即使互联网中断，房屋仍能继续运行。文章还整理了由这一原则延伸出的其他自动化规则和两场相关演讲。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nbcnews.com/tech/tech-news/tesla-wind-cybertruck-production-years-poor-sales-markets-rcna607817" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Tesla to wind down Cybertruck production</span><span class='zh'>特斯拉将逐步停止生产 Cybertruck</span></a><span class="dom">nbcnews.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Tesla CEO Elon Musk said Cybertruck production at the company&#x27;s Texas gigafactory will wind down around mid-2027.</span><span class='zh'>特斯拉 CEO 埃隆·马斯克表示，公司位于得州的超级工厂将在 2027 年年中左右逐步停止生产 Cybertruck。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Tesla CEO Elon Musk said Cybertruck production at the company&#x27;s Texas gigafactory will wind down around mid-2027. He said the move will make room for higher production of the newly launched Cybercab, central to Tesla&#x27;s robotaxi plans. Cybertruck sales have remained weak, and it has sold in only a limited number of markets. It remains unclear whether production will move elsewhere or end entirely.</span><span class='zh'>特斯拉 CEO 埃隆·马斯克表示，公司位于得州的超级工厂将在 2027 年年中左右逐步停止生产 Cybertruck。他称，此举将为提高新款 Cybercab 的产量腾出空间，而 Cybercab 是特斯拉机器人出租车计划的核心车型。Cybertruck 销量长期疲软，目前仅在少数市场销售。该车型是转移到其他工厂生产还是彻底停产，目前尚不清楚。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">09</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mitchellh.com/writing/libghostty-1-0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Libghostty 1.0</span><span class='zh'>Libghostty 1.0</span></a><span class="dom">mitchellh.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>libghostty-vt 1.0 has been released as a portable, high-performance C library for terminal emulation, extracted from Ghostty’s core.</span><span class='zh'>libghostty-vt 1.0 已发布，这是一个从 Ghostty 核心提取出的便携、高性能终端模拟 C 库。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>libghostty-vt 1.0 has been released as a portable, high-performance C library for terminal emulation, extracted from Ghostty’s core. Both the library and its C ABI are now stable. The C API has grown from nonexistent to a complete terminal emulator used in production by millions of people across hundreds of free and commercial products.</span><span class='zh'>libghostty-vt 1.0 已发布，这是一个从 Ghostty 核心提取出的便携、高性能终端模拟 C 库。该库本身及其 C ABI 现已稳定。其 C API 已从无到有发展为完整的终端模拟器，目前已在数百款免费和商业产品中投入生产，服务数百万人。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://pietromiotti.com/latest/maya-site" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://pietromiotti.com/latest/maya-site/forest-vs-lidar.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='pietromiotti.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">10</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://pietromiotti.com/latest/maya-site" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>My AI agents found an unmapped Maya site?</span><span class='zh'>我的 AI 代理发现了一个未标记的玛雅遗址？</span></a><span class="dom">pietromiotti.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI agents analyzed NASA lidar data and identified a Maya site beneath the jungle in Mexico.</span><span class='zh'>AI 代理分析 NASA 的激光雷达数据，在墨西哥丛林下识别出一处玛雅遗址。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI agents analyzed NASA lidar data and identified a Maya site beneath the jungle in Mexico. The site had already been mapped by archaeologists and described in Estrada-Belli et al. 2025, but it was not yet listed in Mexico’s official INAH register. The agents therefore rediscovered the site rather than discovering it, and the process took less than 72 hours to reach an official response from the Mexican government.</span><span class='zh'>AI 代理分析 NASA 的激光雷达数据，在墨西哥丛林下识别出一处玛雅遗址。考古学家其实已经绘制过该遗址的地图，并在 Estrada-Belli 等人 2025 年的论文中记录了它，只是它尚未列入墨西哥国家人类学与历史研究所（INAH）的官方名录。因此，代理完成的是“重新发现”而非发现；从提出想法到收到墨西哥政府的正式回复耗时不到 72 小时。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span><span class='tag' data-t='Space' style='--c:#818cf8'>Space</span><span class='tag' data-t='Politics' style='--c:#f97316'>Politics</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>6.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>929</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50452455' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>338</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50442736" data-id="50442736" data-tags="Web|Legal" data-score="919" data-comments="204" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50442736" data-time="1794328664">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://neil.fraser.name/news/2026/11/10/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>.name Grandfathered</span><span class='zh'>.name 域名获得保留</span></a><span class="dom">neil.fraser.name</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Verisign has withdrawn its plan to terminate 22,000 .name domain names and email redirects after a prolonged campaign.</span><span class='zh'>经过一场旷日持久的抗争，Verisign 撤回了终止 22,000 个 .name 域名及其邮件转发的计划。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Verisign has withdrawn its plan to terminate 22,000 .name domain names and email redirects after a prolonged campaign. New registrations for third-level .name domains will close, but all existing domains and redirects will remain indefinitely. The reversal followed efforts by domain owners, lawyers, and supporters to challenge the plan through ICANN.</span><span class='zh'>经过一场旷日持久的抗争，Verisign 撤回了终止 22,000 个 .name 域名及其邮件转发的计划。虽然 .name 三级域名将停止新注册，但现有域名和邮件转发服务将无限期保留。这一转变源于域名所有者、律师和支持者通过 ICANN 发起的共同争取。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.anthropic.com/claude-fable-and-mythos-5-2" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Fable 5.2 and Mythos 5.2</span><span class='zh'>Claude Fable 5.2 与 Mythos 5.2</span></a><span class="dom">anthropic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic has introduced Claude Fable 5.2 and Claude Mythos 5.2 for demanding technical tasks.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 推出了面向高难度技术任务的 Claude Fable 5.2 和 Claude Mythos 5.2。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic has introduced Claude Fable 5.2 and Claude Mythos 5.2 for demanding technical tasks. The company says the models improve coding, reasoning, and vision, and that prerelease testing helped engineers complete software migrations, design three working electronic devices, advance robotics evaluations, and formalize a proof of Fermat&#x27;s Last Theorem. Fable 5.2 is positioned as a new high point for coding, finance, and science.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 推出了面向高难度技术任务的 Claude Fable 5.2 和 Claude Mythos 5.2。该公司称，这些模型在编程、推理和视觉方面有所提升，并在预发布测试中帮助工程师完成软件迁移、从零设计三种可工作的电子设备、推进机器人评测，以及形式化费马大定理的证明。Fable 5.2 被定位为编程、金融和科学领域的新标杆。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://8rank.ai/game/apple-intelligence-personal-knowledge-graph" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Every Mac comes with an (undocumented) family member classifier</span><span class='zh'>每台 Mac 都自带一个未公开的家庭成员分类器</span></a><span class="dom">8rank.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The post claims that every Mac since macOS 13, released in 2022, includes two daemons that build a graph of the user’s relationships and activities.</span><span class='zh'>文章称，自 2022 年发布的 macOS 13 起，每台 Mac 都包含两个用于构建用户关系和活动图谱的后台进程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The post claims that every Mac since macOS 13, released in 2022, includes two daemons that build a graph of the user’s relationships and activities. The graph is described as covering contacts, relationships, communications, locations, activities, and timing.</span><span class='zh'>文章称，自 2022 年发布的 macOS 13 起，每台 Mac 都包含两个用于构建用户关系和活动图谱的后台进程。该图谱据称涵盖联系人、彼此关系、通信、位置、活动及时间信息。文章还分析了这一系统的工作方式，并提供了查看个人设备上该图谱的工具。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/07/top_stories_11072026'><span class='en'>Nov 7</span><span class='zh'>11月7日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>850</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50403097' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>450</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50429906" data-id="50429906" data-tags="AI|Science" data-score="839" data-comments="507" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50429906" data-time="1794250589">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.anthropic.com/claude-fable-and-mythos-5-2" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www-cdn.anthropic.com/images/4zrzovbb/website/b2dab182c77b4ccfd36423470106915b20f676ae-1200x630.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='anthropic.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.anthropic.com/claude-fable-and-mythos-5-2" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Fable 5.2 and Mythos 5.2</span><span class='zh'>Claude Fable 5.2 与 Mythos 5.2</span></a><span class="dom">anthropic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic introduced Claude Fable 5.2 and Claude Mythos 5.2 for demanding technical work, with claimed improvements in coding, reasoning, and vision.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 推出了面向高难度技术工作的 Claude Fable 5.2 和 Claude Mythos 5.2，并称它们在编码、推理和视觉能力方面有所提升。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic introduced Claude Fable 5.2 and Claude Mythos 5.2 for demanding technical work, with claimed improvements in coding, reasoning, and vision. During prerelease testing, Anthropic says the models helped engineers complete software migrations, design three working electronic devices, make progress on robotics evaluations, and solve problems in physics. The company also says the models formalized a proof of Fermat’s Last Theorem in 30 hours.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 推出了面向高难度技术工作的 Claude Fable 5.2 和 Claude Mythos 5.2，并称它们在编码、推理和视觉能力方面有所提升。在预发布测试中，Anthropic 表示这些模型帮助工程师完成软件迁移、设计出三种可运行的电子设备、推进机器人评测，并解决物理学问题。公司还称，模型在 30 小时内形式化完成了费马大定理的证明。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.bosscher.ch/posts/openstick/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>What I run on my $10 USD OpenStick homelab</span><span class='zh'>我在 10 美元 OpenStick 家庭实验室上运行的服务</span></a><span class="dom">blog.bosscher.ch</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author bought an OpenStick for $10 including shipping, mainly because it was an unusual and appealing piece of hardware.</span><span class='zh'>作者花 10 美元（含运费）买了一台 OpenStick，主要是因为这款硬件新奇而有趣。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author bought an OpenStick for $10 including shipping, mainly because it was an unusual and appealing piece of hardware. Although the device is less powerful than many modern watches, it can still support enjoyable self-hosting experiments. The project demonstrates how a very inexpensive, tiny computer can serve as an entry point to homelabbing.</span><span class='zh'>作者花 10 美元（含运费）买了一台 OpenStick，主要是因为这款硬件新奇而有趣。它的性能可能还不如许多现代智能手表，但仍然适合进行有趣的自托管实验。这个项目展示了廉价小设备如何成为入门家庭实验室的起点。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://caleb.software/posts/claude-to-pi.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Goodbye Claude, hello Pi</span><span class='zh'>告别 Claude，迎接 Pi</span></a><span class="dom">caleb.software</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author had used Claude Code for more than a year, beginning when it became available through the Claude Pro subscription.</span><span class='zh'>作者从 Claude Code 随 Claude Pro 订阅推出后就开始使用，至今已有一年多。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author had used Claude Code for more than a year, beginning when it became available through the Claude Pro subscription. Despite considering its release an important moment for software development, the author accumulated enough small frustrations to stop using it as the main coding harness. Their development setup is now being migrated to Pi.</span><span class='zh'>作者从 Claude Code 随 Claude Pro 订阅推出后就开始使用，至今已有一年多。尽管作者认为它的发布可能会成为软件开发史上的重要节点，但长期积累的许多小问题最终让作者不再把它作为主要编程工具。作者目前正将开发环境迁移到 Pi。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ppmpreetham/BUI" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Blender&#x27;s UI and Runtime: Much Faster Than GPUI</span><span class='zh'>Blender 的 UI 与运行时：比 GPUI 快得多</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>BUI is a GPU-accelerated Blender user interface decoupled from Blender&#x27;s original codebase.</span><span class='zh'>BUI 是一个与 Blender 原始代码库解耦的 GPU 加速用户界面。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>BUI is a GPU-accelerated Blender user interface decoupled from Blender&#x27;s original codebase. It supports Rust, Python, JavaScript, React, C, and C++. The project describes its performance as twice that of GPUI.</span><span class='zh'>BUI 是一个与 Blender 原始代码库解耦的 GPU 加速用户界面。它支持 Rust、Python、JavaScript、React、C 和 C++。项目方称其性能是 GPUI 的两倍。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://postalpro.usps.com/zcb" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>USPS now offers official ZIP Code boundary polygons</span><span class='zh'>USPS 现提供官方 ZIP Code 边界多边形</span></a><span class="dom">postalpro.usps.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The USPS has released a national ZIP Code boundary map built from delivery-address and location data.</span><span class='zh'>美国邮政服务（USPS）发布了基于投递地址和位置数据制作的全国 ZIP Code 边界地图。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The USPS has released a national ZIP Code boundary map built from delivery-address and location data. The boundaries can change as delivery locations are added or modified, and the data is updated quarterly with limited distribution to subsidiaries and partners. The dataset is intended for mapping, mailing, marketing, insurance, real estate, logistics, navigation, and transportation applications.</span><span class='zh'>美国邮政服务（USPS）发布了基于投递地址和位置数据制作的全国 ZIP Code 边界地图。随着投递地点新增或变更，边界可能发生变化；数据每季度更新一次，并仅向子公司和合作伙伴有限提供。该数据可用于地图、邮寄、营销、保险、房地产、物流、导航和交通等行业。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Data' style='--c:#6366f1'>Data</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/09/top_stories_11092026'><span class='en'>Nov 9</span><span class='zh'>11月9日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>720</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50431058' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>388</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50429426" data-id="50429426" data-tags="Privacy|Security|AI|Code" data-score="716" data-comments="147" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50429426" data-time="1794248678">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">20</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/thisisme6598/gemini_files/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Google Gemini routes personal Drive files and emails to human annotators</span><span class='zh'>Google Gemini 将个人 Drive 文件和邮件交由人工标注员处理</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Updated Google guidance reportedly confirms that content from connected Drive and email accounts can enter human-review pipelines for Gemini.</span><span class='zh'>Google 更新后的说明据称确认，连接到 Gemini 的 Drive 和电子邮件账户内容可能进入人工审核流程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Updated Google guidance reportedly confirms that content from connected Drive and email accounts can enter human-review pipelines for Gemini. This reverses a 2023 promise that personal Gmail, Docs, and Drive content would not be seen by human reviewers. Google now warns users not to connect apps containing confidential information they would not want a reviewer to see.</span><span class='zh'>Google 更新后的说明据称确认，连接到 Gemini 的 Drive 和电子邮件账户内容可能进入人工审核流程。这改变了 Google 在 2023 年作出的承诺——当时公司表示个人 Gmail、Docs 和 Drive 内容不会被人工审核员看到。Google 目前警告用户，不要连接包含不希望审核员看到的机密信息的应用。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/09/top_stories_11092026'><span class='en'>Nov 9</span><span class='zh'>11月9日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>716</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50429426' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>147</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50363461" data-id="50363461" data-tags="Privacy|AI" data-score="715" data-comments="189" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50363461" data-time="1793787027">
<a class="thumb" href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50363461" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=news.ycombinator.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>news.ycombinator.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">21</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50363461" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Tell HN: Gemini trains on chat history, even for subscribers (personal accounts)</span><span class='zh'>告诉 HN：Gemini 会用聊天记录训练，即使是订阅用户的个人账户也不例外</span></a><span class="dom">news.ycombinator.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>For personal Google AI subscriptions, saved Gemini chats can be used to improve Google services and train generative AI models when Keep Activity is enabled.</span><span class='zh'>对于个人 Google AI 订阅账户，在启用“保留活动记录”时，保存的 Gemini 聊天记录可用于改进 Google 服务和训练生成式 AI 模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>For personal Google AI subscriptions, saved Gemini chats can be used to improve Google services and train generative AI models when Keep Activity is enabled. A subset of chats may be reviewed by human reviewers and can be retained for up to three years after review, even if the user deletes activity. Users are warned not to enter confidential information, and opting out requires disabling chat history or using temporary chats.</span><span class='zh'>对于个人 Google AI 订阅账户，在启用“保留活动记录”时，保存的 Gemini 聊天记录可用于改进 Google 服务和训练生成式 AI 模型。部分聊天可能由人工审核，审核过的内容即使用户删除活动记录，也可能保留最长三年。用户被提醒不要输入机密信息；若要退出，需要关闭聊天记录或使用临时聊天。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/04/top_stories_11042026'><span class='en'>Nov 4</span><span class='zh'>11月4日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>715</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50363461' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>189</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50422069" data-id="50422069" data-tags="AI" data-score="708" data-comments="459" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50422069" data-time="1794207865">
<a class="thumb" href="https://twitter.com/shanemelaugh/status/2119192075505893695" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HWjhsqYWMAALhKb?format=webp&amp;name=large' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='twitter.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">22</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://twitter.com/shanemelaugh/status/2119192075505893695" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Is Opus 5.5 nerfed? Thinking patterns changed on November 3</span><span class='zh'>Opus 5.5 被削弱了吗？11 月 3 日推理模式发生变化</span></a><span class="dom">twitter.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An analysis of 1,400 Claude Code sessions reportedly found a sharp change in Opus 5.5’s reasoning behavior on November 3.</span><span class='zh'>对 1400 次 Claude Code 会话的分析据称发现，Opus 5.5 在 11 月 3 日的推理行为发生了明显变化。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An analysis of 1,400 Claude Code sessions reportedly found a sharp change in Opus 5.5’s reasoning behavior on November 3. The model name did not change, but the analysis claims that its thinking patterns shifted noticeably. The report does not identify the cause of the change.</span><span class='zh'>对 1400 次 Claude Code 会话的分析据称发现，Opus 5.5 在 11 月 3 日的推理行为发生了明显变化。模型名称并未改变，但分析认为其思考模式出现了显著转折。该报告没有说明变化原因。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/09/top_stories_11092026'><span class='en'>Nov 9</span><span class='zh'>11月9日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>3.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>708</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50422069' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>459</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50415628" data-id="50415628" data-tags="AI|Programming|Finance" data-score="707" data-comments="391" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50415628" data-time="1794161767">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.thestateofai.com/news/goldman-sachs-claude-code-traders-bankers" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.thestateofai.com/media/uploads/33f913ce812c4074d207c01b6df1858a692e6bc3229dadbfcd4f56b61722a538.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='thestateofai.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">23</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.thestateofai.com/news/goldman-sachs-claude-code-traders-bankers" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Goldman Sachs is pulling Claude Code from its bankers and traders</span><span class='zh'>高盛将从银行家和交易员手中收回 Claude Code</span></a><span class="dom">thestateofai.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Goldman Sachs has restricted Anthropic&#x27;s Claude Code and Microsoft&#x27;s GitHub to its engineers, removing access for most traders and bankers.</span><span class='zh'>高盛已将 Anthropic 的 Claude Code 和微软 GitHub 的使用权限限制在工程师范围内，大多数交易员和银行家因此失去访问权限。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Goldman Sachs has restricted Anthropic&#x27;s Claude Code and Microsoft&#x27;s GitHub to its engineers, removing access for most traders and bankers. Those employees had used the tools to track client flows and build market models, while the bank had already barred Hong Kong bankers from Claude in April. The guidance followed a review of productivity and risk concerns and came weeks before Goldman was due to act as a lead underwriter for Anthropic&#x27;s IPO.</span><span class='zh'>高盛已将 Anthropic 的 Claude Code 和微软 GitHub 的使用权限限制在工程师范围内，大多数交易员和银行家因此失去访问权限。此前，这些员工曾用相关工具追踪客户资金流向并构建市场模型；高盛也已于 4 月禁止香港银行家使用 Claude。这项指导是在审查生产力和风险问题后出台的，时间上距离高盛担任 Anthropic IPO 主承销商仅数周。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Finance' style='--c:#84cc16'>Finance</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/08/top_stories_11082026'><span class='en'>Nov 8</span><span class='zh'>11月8日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>5.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>707</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50415628' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>391</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50384233" data-id="50384233" data-tags="Security" data-score="704" data-comments="276" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50384233" data-time="1793911422">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.cve.org/CVERecord?id=CVE-2026-83844" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>MS Paint Remote Code Execution Vulnerability</span><span class='zh'>MS Paint 远程代码执行漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">cve.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A remote code execution vulnerability has been reported in Microsoft Paint.</span><span class='zh'>微软画图工具被报告存在远程代码执行漏洞。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A remote code execution vulnerability has been reported in Microsoft Paint. The issue could allow code to run remotely when exploited.</span><span class='zh'>微软画图工具被报告存在远程代码执行漏洞。攻击者利用该问题后，可能在远程执行代码。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://strandsagents.com/blog/amazon-saves-millions-replacing-sonnet-with-strands-decider/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Amazon saves millions by replacing Sonnet 4.6 with Strands Decider</span><span class='zh'>亚马逊用 Strands Decider 替代 Sonnet 4.6，节省数百万美元</span></a><span class="dom">strandsagents.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Two Amazon engineers replaced Claude Sonnet 4.6 with Strands Decider in a major internal ticketing system.</span><span class='zh'>亚马逊的两名工程师在一个大型内部工单系统中，用 Strands Decider 替代了 Claude Sonnet 4.6。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Two Amazon engineers replaced Claude Sonnet 4.6 with Strands Decider in a major internal ticketing system. Sonnet had provided a balance of intelligence and cost, but Strands Decider reportedly performed equally well at 98% lower cost. The change is expected to save millions of dollars in annual token expenses.</span><span class='zh'>亚马逊的两名工程师在一个大型内部工单系统中，用 Strands Decider 替代了 Claude Sonnet 4.6。Sonnet 原本在智能程度和成本之间取得了平衡，但据称 Strands Decider 在表现相当的情况下将成本降低了 98%。这一调整预计每年可节省数百万美元的 token 费用。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://kagi.com/changelog#11820" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Search for everyone</span><span class='zh'>面向所有人的搜索</span></a><span class="dom">kagi.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Kagi has opened web search to anyone without requiring an account for the first 50 searches.</span><span class='zh'>Kagi 现在允许任何人使用网页搜索，前 50 次搜索无需创建账户。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Kagi has opened web search to anyone without requiring an account for the first 50 searches. Registering adds another 100 searches. The paid search engine says it does not run ads and is relying on community recommendations and word of mouth.</span><span class='zh'>Kagi 现在允许任何人使用网页搜索，前 50 次搜索无需创建账户。注册后还可额外获得 100 次搜索。这个付费搜索引擎表示自己不投放广告，而是依靠社区推荐和口碑传播。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">28</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/PrivSec-dev/banking-apps-compat-report/issues/105" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Dutch &#x27;idealist&#x27; bank ASN blocks non-Google Android, including GrapheneOS</span><span class='zh'>荷兰“理想主义”银行 ASN 屏蔽非 Google Android，包括 GrapheneOS</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A compatibility report examines whether the ASN Bank Android app blocks non-Google Android systems, including GrapheneOS.</span><span class='zh'>一份兼容性报告调查 ASN Bank 的 Android 应用是否屏蔽包括 GrapheneOS 在内的非 Google Android 系统。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A compatibility report examines whether the ASN Bank Android app blocks non-Google Android systems, including GrapheneOS. The report identifies app version 2.40.2 in the Netherlands. One user reported that the app appeared to work on a stock operating system.</span><span class='zh'>一份兼容性报告调查 ASN Bank 的 Android 应用是否屏蔽包括 GrapheneOS 在内的非 Google Android 系统。报告涉及荷兰地区的 2.40.2 版本。一名用户表示，该应用在原生操作系统上看起来可以正常运行。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/06/top_stories_11062026'><span class='en'>Nov 6</span><span class='zh'>11月6日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>648</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50390124' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>424</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50366999" data-id="50366999" data-tags="AI|Open Source|Code" data-score="627" data-comments="186" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50366999" data-time="1793806230">
<a class="thumb" href="https://huggingface.co/moonshotai/Kimi-K3.1-Pro" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://cdn-thumbnails.huggingface.co/social-thumbnails/models/moonshotai/Kimi-K3.1-Pro.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='huggingface.co' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">29</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://huggingface.co/moonshotai/Kimi-K3.1-Pro" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Kimi K3.1 Pro model weights are out</span><span class='zh'>Kimi K3.1 Pro 模型权重已发布</span></a><span class="dom">huggingface.co</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Moonshot AI has released the weights for Kimi K3.1 Pro, an open-weight, natively multimodal agentic model with 2.8 trillion parameters and a one-million-token context window.</span><span class='zh'>月之暗面发布了 Kimi K3.1 Pro 的模型权重。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Moonshot AI has released the weights for Kimi K3.1 Pro, an open-weight, natively multimodal agentic model with 2.8 trillion parameters and a one-million-token context window. It was post-trained from Kimi K3 using large-scale joint reinforcement learning across hundreds of domains. The model replaces Gated MLA with Gated DeepSeek Sparse Attention for more efficient long-context modeling.</span><span class='zh'>月之暗面发布了 Kimi K3.1 Pro 的模型权重。这是一款开放权重、原生多模态的智能体模型，拥有 2.8 万亿参数和 100 万 token 的上下文窗口。该模型基于 Kimi K3，并通过覆盖数百个领域的大规模联合强化学习进行后训练；同时以 Gated DeepSeek Sparse Attention 替代 Gated MLA，以提高长上下文建模效率。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/04/top_stories_11042026'><span class='en'>Nov 4</span><span class='zh'>11月4日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>627</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50366999' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>186</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50379704" data-id="50379704" data-tags="Security" data-score="621" data-comments="2" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50379704" data-time="1793889860">
<a class="thumb" href="https://alpinelinux.org/posts/2026-11-05-openssh-password-authentication-bypass/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ef4444' data-tag='Security'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=alpinelinux.org&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>alpinelinux.org</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">30</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://alpinelinux.org/posts/2026-11-05-openssh-password-authentication-bypass/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Alpine Linux Security Advisory: OpenSSH-server password authentication bypass</span><span class='zh'>Alpine Linux 安全公告：OpenSSH-server 绕过密码认证</span></a><span class="dom">alpinelinux.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Alpine Linux advises users running sshd to update openssh immediately and restart the daemon.</span><span class='zh'>Alpine Linux 建议运行 sshd 的用户立即更新 openssh 并重启守护进程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Alpine Linux advises users running sshd to update openssh immediately and restart the daemon. Updating the package alone does not replace an already running sshd process. Until updating is possible, disabling password logins with PasswordAuthentication no and restarting sshd closes the hole.</span><span class='zh'>Alpine Linux 建议运行 sshd 的用户立即更新 openssh 并重启守护进程。仅更新软件包并不会替换已经运行的 sshd 进程。在完成更新前，可将配置设为 PasswordAuthentication no 并重启 sshd，以关闭该漏洞。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.mindstudio.ai/blog/linux-nvidia-rtx-spark-laptop" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://i.mscdn.ai/o/iZl0kkZU2R9KXyy7/a/3V2ICO3oSq2QZPii/_published/generated-images/0d8c9668-cbfe-4ecf-bb31-1a7fff7102be.png?fm=auto&amp;w=1200&amp;h=630&amp;fit=crop' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='mindstudio.ai' data-c='#f59e0b' data-tag='Hardware'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.mindstudio.ai/blog/linux-nvidia-rtx-spark-laptop" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nvidia RTX Spark encrypted firmware slows inference speed to 35% on Linux</span><span class='zh'>英伟达 RTX Spark 在 Linux 上因加密固件导致推理速度降至 35%</span></a><span class="dom">mindstudio.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>RTX Spark laptops run Linux with GPU acceleration through Nvidia’s open kernel driver, but their GPU clocks drop to roughly 780 MHz when plugged in, instead of exceeding 2 GHz on Windows.</span><span class='zh'>RTX Spark 笔记本可以在 Linux 上运行，并通过英伟达开源内核驱动实现 GPU 加速，但接通电源后 GPU 频率会被限制在约 780MHz，而 Windows 上可超过 2GHz。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>RTX Spark laptops run Linux with GPU acceleration through Nvidia’s open kernel driver, but their GPU clocks drop to roughly 780 MHz when plugged in, instead of exceeding 2 GHz on Windows. On a 120-billion-parameter model, performance falls from about 65 tokens per second to fewer than 14. The problem is tied to power-management firmware that Linux currently cannot read.</span><span class='zh'>RTX Spark 笔记本可以在 Linux 上运行，并通过英伟达开源内核驱动实现 GPU 加速，但接通电源后 GPU 频率会被限制在约 780MHz，而 Windows 上可超过 2GHz。在一个 1200 亿参数模型上，速度因此从约每秒 65 个 token 降至不足 14 个。问题与 Linux 目前无法读取的电源管理固件有关。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/04/top_stories_11042026'><span class='en'>Nov 4</span><span class='zh'>11月4日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>612</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50362905' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>287</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50407882" data-id="50407882" data-tags="Security|AI|Programming" data-score="599" data-comments="408" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50407882" data-time="1794084332">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://apiiro.com/blog/coding-agents-leaking-secrets-github-dont-ask-again" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Code&#x27;s &quot;Don&#x27;t Ask Again&quot; exposes passwords in 1,122 public GitHub files</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 的“不要再询问”功能导致密码出现在 1,122 个公开 GitHub 文件中</span></a><span class="dom">apiiro.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Claude Code&#x27;s &quot;Don&#x27;t Ask Again&quot; feature exposed passwords in 1,122 public GitHub files.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 的“不要再询问”功能导致密码出现在 1,122 个公开 GitHub 文件中。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Claude Code&#x27;s &quot;Don&#x27;t Ask Again&quot; feature exposed passwords in 1,122 public GitHub files.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 的“不要再询问”功能导致密码出现在 1,122 个公开 GitHub 文件中。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://deepmind.google/blog/introducing-alphaproof-code/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/hn6AiI6lYFo6chi1mL6uJ9nOW9UXfsAKnSyOApiie2urHNU2tmSPUmGn0hNfWyYLRcsWCGjCIrBZSCt1ihZmvW-LDJPfyolsr5w-yvzlknZVzmaH=w1200-h630-n-nu-rw' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='deepmind.google' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://deepmind.google/blog/introducing-alphaproof-code/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AlphaProof Code</span><span class='zh'>AlphaProof Code</span></a><span class="dom">deepmind.google</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AlphaProof Code agents generated more than three million lines of machine-checked Lean proof.</span><span class='zh'>AlphaProof Code 代理生成了超过 300 万行经过机器检查的 Lean 证明。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AlphaProof Code agents generated more than three million lines of machine-checked Lean proof. The proofs verify Signal’s core cryptographic protections down to machine instructions. The result demonstrates the agents’ use in formal verification of security-critical software.</span><span class='zh'>AlphaProof Code 代理生成了超过 300 万行经过机器检查的 Lean 证明。这些证明将 Signal 的核心加密防护验证到了机器指令级别。该成果展示了这类代理在安全关键软件形式化验证中的用途。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/10/top_stories_11102026'><span class='en'>Nov 10</span><span class='zh'>11月10日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>3.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>590</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50439193' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>179</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50427362" data-id="50427362" data-tags="Finance" data-score="579" data-comments="342" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50427362" data-time="1794240045">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.payments.ca/canada-launches-real-time-rail-bringing-247-payment-infrastructure-national-economy" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.payments.ca/sites/default/files/2026-11/CanadaLightsUp.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='payments.ca' data-c='#84cc16' data-tag='Finance'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.payments.ca/canada-launches-real-time-rail-bringing-247-payment-infrastructure-national-economy" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Payments Canada announces launch of Canada&#x27;s Real-Time Rail (RTR) payment system</span><span class='zh'>Payments Canada 宣布推出加拿大实时铁路支付系统</span></a><span class="dom">payments.ca</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Payments Canada announced the launch of Canada’s Real-Time Rail, a national payment system for instant clearing and settlement.</span><span class='zh'>Payments Canada 宣布推出加拿大实时铁路（RTR），这是一个支持即时清算和结算的全国性支付系统。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Payments Canada announced the launch of Canada’s Real-Time Rail, a national payment system for instant clearing and settlement. Participating financial institutions, credit unions, and payment service providers can exchange, clear, and settle payments securely in real time, 24 hours a day. The system also provides real-time cash-flow visibility and data-rich messaging through existing banking and payment channels.</span><span class='zh'>Payments Canada 宣布推出加拿大实时铁路（RTR），这是一个支持即时清算和结算的全国性支付系统。参与其中的金融机构、信用合作社和支付服务商可以全年全天候安全地实时交换、清算和结算付款。该系统还通过现有银行和支付渠道提供实时现金流可见性以及包含丰富数据的消息传递。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.tomshardware.com/pc-components/cpus/amd-expects-to-outsell-intel-in-q4-for-the-first-time-in-history-usd17b-guidance-would-exceed-intels-projections" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AMD expects to outsell Intel in Q4 for the first time in history</span><span class='zh'>AMD 预计第四季度历史首次超过英特尔</span></a><span class="dom">tomshardware.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AMD expects to outsell Intel in the fourth quarter for the first time in its history.</span><span class='zh'>AMD 预计将在第四季度历史上首次超过英特尔。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AMD expects to outsell Intel in the fourth quarter for the first time in its history. Record quarterly revenue is being driven by the AI boom, while its client and gaming businesses are facing double-digit declines heading into 2027. The forecast indicates a widening contrast between AMD’s AI growth and its weaker traditional segments.</span><span class='zh'>AMD 预计将在第四季度历史上首次超过英特尔。AI 热潮推动其季度营收创下纪录，但面向客户的业务和游戏业务在进入 2027 年时正面临两位数下滑。这一预测显示，AMD 的 AI 业务增长与传统业务疲软之间的差距正在扩大。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span><span class='tag' data-t='Finance' style='--c:#84cc16'>Finance</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Gaming' style='--c:#a855f7'>Gaming</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/04/top_stories_11042026'><span class='en'>Nov 4</span><span class='zh'>11月4日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>578</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50367125' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>221</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50389222" data-id="50389222" data-tags="Politics" data-score="577" data-comments="437" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50389222" data-time="1793949724">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.reuters.com/business/world-at-work/britain-make-salary-disclosure-job-ads-mandatory-2026-11-05/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Britain to make salary disclosure in job ads mandatory</span><span class='zh'>英国将强制要求招聘广告披露薪资</span></a><span class="dom">reuters.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Britain plans to make salary disclosure mandatory in job advertisements.</span><span class='zh'>英国计划强制招聘广告披露薪资信息。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Britain plans to make salary disclosure mandatory in job advertisements. Employers would have to include pay information when advertising open positions.</span><span class='zh'>英国计划强制招聘广告披露薪资信息。雇主发布职位空缺时将必须列出薪酬信息。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Politics' style='--c:#f97316'>Politics</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/05/top_stories_11052026'><span class='en'>Nov 5</span><span class='zh'>11月5日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>577</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50389222' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>437</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50397429" data-id="50397429" data-tags="AI|Data" data-score="574" data-comments="146" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50397429" data-time="1793994531">
<a class="thumb" href="https://hightouch.com/blog/llm-judges-identity-resolution" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/pwmfmi47/production/9a5d7191d614c824c822ec90672adaf424699d3d-2400x1256.png?w=1200' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='hightouch.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">37</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://hightouch.com/blog/llm-judges-identity-resolution" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A Jev that can ask Opus for help gives Opus accuracy at 4x lower cost</span><span class='zh'>能向 Opus 求助的 Jev，让 Opus 以低四倍成本达到同等准确率</span></a><span class="dom">hightouch.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Tests of Jev and GPT-6 Luna as judges for identity resolution found out-of-the-box accuracy ranging from 88.8% to 97.3%.</span><span class='zh'>对 Jev 和 GPT-6 Luna 作为身份解析评判器的测试显示，它们开箱即用的准确率为 88.8% 至 97.3%。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Tests of Jev and GPT-6 Luna as judges for identity resolution found out-of-the-box accuracy ranging from 88.8% to 97.3%. A hybrid that combines Jev with Claude Opus matched frontier-level accuracy at more than four times lower cost. Identity resolution maps data records to unique real-world people.</span><span class='zh'>对 Jev 和 GPT-6 Luna 作为身份解析评判器的测试显示，它们开箱即用的准确率为 88.8% 至 97.3%。将 Jev 与 Claude Opus 结合的混合方案达到了前沿模型水平的准确率，而成本降低了四倍以上。身份解析的任务是将数据记录映射到现实世界中唯一的个人。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.salon.com/2026/11/05/goodbye-from-salon/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Goodbye from Salon</span><span class='zh'>Salon 告别</span></a><span class="dom">salon.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Salon has announced that it will cease publication after 31 years.</span><span class='zh'>Salon 宣布，在出版 31 年后将停止运营。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Salon has announced that it will cease publication after 31 years. The publication described its history as an attempt to explore what journalism could become while the internet was still new.</span><span class='zh'>Salon 宣布，在出版 31 年后将停止运营。该出版物将自己的历史描述为一次探索新闻业可能形态的尝试，起步于互联网仍处于早期阶段之时。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/05/top_stories_11052026'><span class='en'>Nov 5</span><span class='zh'>11月5日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>4.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>557</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50383739' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>212</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50445401" data-id="50445401" data-tags="Business" data-score="553" data-comments="397" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50445401" data-time="1794340246">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theautopian.com/scion-is-back-and-its-going-to-sell-a-freaking-kei-truck-in-america/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Scion is back and is going to sell a kei truck in America</span><span class='zh'>Scion 回归，将在美国销售一款 Kei 微型卡车</span></a><span class="dom">theautopian.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Toyota has officially revived the Scion marque, which was retired in 2016 after years of financial struggles.</span><span class='zh'>丰田正式复活了 Scion 品牌；该品牌曾因长期经营困难于 2016 年停运。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Toyota has officially revived the Scion marque, which was retired in 2016 after years of financial struggles. The new “Scion by Toyota” is being positioned as a “freedom lab” focused on challenging conventional automotive thinking, and its initial offerings include a kei truck, a bike, and a hybrid side-by-side. The brand’s new direction is a sharp departure from its former role as a quirky, budget-oriented line aimed largely at younger buyers.</span><span class='zh'>丰田正式复活了 Scion 品牌；该品牌曾因长期经营困难于 2016 年停运。全新的“Scion by Toyota”被定位为一个挑战传统汽车思维的“自由实验室”，首批产品包括一款 Kei 微型卡车、一辆自行车和一款混合动力并排式越野车。品牌的新方向与过去面向年轻消费者、主打古怪且价格亲民车型的定位大相径庭。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theguardian.com/technology/2026/nov/06/struggling-to-spell-male-chauvinist-pig-apple-grey-list-means-your-iphone-wont-help-you" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Apple’s grey list: Words and phrases your iPhone won’t help you spell</span><span class='zh'>苹果的“灰名单”：iPhone 不会帮你拼出的词语和短语</span></a><span class="dom">theguardian.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Research found that Apple restricts predictive-text completion for more than 100,000 words and phrases.</span><span class='zh'>研究发现，苹果限制了超过 10 万个词语和短语的预测文本补全功能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Research found that Apple restricts predictive-text completion for more than 100,000 words and phrases. The restrictions affect swipe typing, in which users glide a finger across the keyboard instead of tapping each character. As a result, an iPhone may refuse to complete some unusual or sensitive phrases even when users try to enter them by gesture.</span><span class='zh'>研究发现，苹果限制了超过 10 万个词语和短语的预测文本补全功能。这些限制会影响滑行输入，即用户用手指划过键盘，而不是逐字点击。结果是，即使用户尝试通过滑行输入，iPhone 也可能不会补全某些不寻常或敏感的短语。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50366117" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ask HN: What&#x27;s your current AI agentic setup?</span><span class='zh'>Ask HN：你目前的 AI agent 使用配置是什么？</span></a><span class="dom">news.ycombinator.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An Ask HN thread asks how people use AI agents in their daily work.</span><span class='zh'>一则 Ask HN 帖子询问人们目前如何在日常工作中使用 AI agent。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An Ask HN thread asks how people use AI agents in their daily work. It solicits details about paid subscriptions, external or locally hosted models, coding harnesses, orchestration tools, and broader agent platforms. The discussion aims to gauge how individual workflows have changed over the past year.</span><span class='zh'>一则 Ask HN 帖子询问人们目前如何在日常工作中使用 AI agent。发帖者希望了解大家购买的订阅、外部或本地部署的模型、编程工具链、编排工具以及更广泛的 agent 平台。讨论旨在了解个人工作流在过去一年中的变化。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://9to5mac.com/2026/11/10/apple-has-new-mac-app-coming-to-make-running-local-ai-models-easy/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Apple has new Mac app coming to make running local AI models easy</span><span class='zh'>Apple 将推出让本地运行 AI 模型更简单的 Mac 应用</span></a><span class="dom">9to5mac.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Apple is reportedly developing a Mac app called Harbor to simplify running local AI models across one or more Macs.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，Apple 正在开发一款名为 Harbor 的 Mac 应用，用于简化在一台或多台 Mac 上运行本地 AI 模型的过程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Apple is reportedly developing a Mac app called Harbor to simplify running local AI models across one or more Macs. Evidence of the app was found by leaker Aaron Perris. The project comes as Macs are increasingly being used to run models locally, including on the Mac Studio and Mac mini.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，Apple 正在开发一款名为 Harbor 的 Mac 应用，用于简化在一台或多台 Mac 上运行本地 AI 模型的过程。泄密者 Aaron Perris 发现了这款应用的相关证据。随着 Mac Studio、Mac mini 等设备被越来越多地用于本地运行模型，该项目正处于这一趋势之中。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://finance.yahoo.com/technology/ai/articles/mark-cuban-says-facebook-knowingly-213000020.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Cuban: Facebook knowingly runs AI deepfake scam ads; docs say $16B fraud revenue</span><span class='zh'>Cuban：Facebook 明知存在 AI 深度伪造诈骗广告，文件称其带来 160 亿美元欺诈收入</span></a><span class="dom">finance.yahoo.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Mark Cuban says AI-generated scam ads have used his face to promote a diabetes cure he never sold.</span><span class='zh'>马克·库班表示，利用其肖像的 AI 生成诈骗广告正在推广他从未销售过的糖尿病疗法。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mark Cuban says AI-generated scam ads have used his face to promote a diabetes cure he never sold. He says people contact him every week believing they bought the supposed cure for $294. The claims come amid allegations that Facebook knowingly runs such deepfake scam advertising.</span><span class='zh'>马克·库班表示，利用其肖像的 AI 生成诈骗广告正在推广他从未销售过的糖尿病疗法。他说，每周都有人联系他，以为自己花 294 美元买到了这种所谓疗法。这些说法出现之际，Facebook 被指控明知相关深度伪造诈骗广告存在却仍在投放。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/05/top_stories_11052026'><span class='en'>Nov 5</span><span class='zh'>11月5日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>505</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50385870' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>224</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50444129" data-id="50444129" data-tags="Web|Business|Programming|Gaming" data-score="502" data-comments="585" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50444129" data-time="1794334952">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://starlink.com/mini-2" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Starlink Mini 2</span><span class='zh'>Starlink Mini 2</span></a><span class="dom">starlink.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Starlink Mini 2 is presented as providing fast, reliable internet both at home and on the go.</span><span class='zh'>Starlink Mini 2 被定位为可在家中和移动途中提供快速、可靠的互联网连接。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Starlink Mini 2 is presented as providing fast, reliable internet both at home and on the go. The service is intended to support streaming, video calls, gaming, and other high-speed connectivity needs, including in rural and remote locations.</span><span class='zh'>Starlink Mini 2 被定位为可在家中和移动途中提供快速、可靠的互联网连接。该服务支持流媒体、视频通话、游戏等高速联网需求，也面向乡村和偏远地区。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft Research&#x27;s AI Frontiers group is coming to an end.</span><span class='zh'>微软研究院的 AI Frontiers 团队即将解散。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft Research&#x27;s AI Frontiers group is coming to an end.</span><span class='zh'>微软研究院的 AI Frontiers 团队即将解散。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://e-means.github.io/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://e-means.github.io/assets/emeans_vs_lloyd_readme_v9.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='e-means.github.io' data-c='#6366f1' data-tag='Data'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://e-means.github.io/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>E-means: Outperforming k-means in one-tenth the epochs</span><span class='zh'>E-means：用十分之一的训练轮次超越 k-means</span></a><span class="dom">e-means.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>E-means is presented as an alternative to Lloyd&#x27;s k-means algorithm.</span><span class='zh'>E-means 被介绍为 Lloyd k-means 算法的一种替代方案。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>E-means is presented as an alternative to Lloyd&#x27;s k-means algorithm. Across a broad range of scales and datasets, it reached lower test loss than Lloyd&#x27;s method in 1.2 to 3 epochs, compared with 25 epochs. The reported end-to-end speedup is 10–45× over Flash-KMeans on GPUs and 8–15× over Faiss on CPUs.</span><span class='zh'>E-means 被介绍为 Lloyd k-means 算法的一种替代方案。在多种规模和数据集上，E-means 用 1.2 至 3 个训练轮次就达到了低于 Lloyd 方法的测试损失，而后者需要 25 个轮次。报告称，端到端速度相比 GPU 上的 Flash-KMeans 提升 10–45 倍，相比 CPU 上的 Faiss 提升 8–15 倍。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Data' style='--c:#6366f1'>Data</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/09/top_stories_11092026'><span class='en'>Nov 9</span><span class='zh'>11月9日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>494</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50429734' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>78</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50443824" data-id="50443824" data-tags="Legal" data-score="481" data-comments="267" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50443824" data-time="1794333722">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://lwn.net/SubscriberLink/1102549/acbc92e00cee3a3f/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Synaptics patent claims stop work on DisplayLink drivers</span><span class='zh'>Synaptics 的专利主张叫停 DisplayLink 驱动开发</span></a><span class="dom">lwn.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Mike Lothian tried to improve a Linux driver for a DisplayLink docking station after finding its performance inadequate.</span><span class='zh'>Mike Lothian 认为 DisplayLink 扩展坞在 Linux 下性能不佳，因此尝试改进其驱动。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mike Lothian tried to improve a Linux driver for a DisplayLink docking station after finding its performance inadequate. Synaptics, which acquired DisplayLink in 2022, responded with patent-infringement claims, a demand to stop the unauthorized implementation, and warnings of costly legal penalties. Linux users of the company’s docks therefore appear likely to remain without feature parity for now.</span><span class='zh'>Mike Lothian 认为 DisplayLink 扩展坞在 Linux 下性能不佳，因此尝试改进其驱动。DisplayLink 于 2022 年被 Synaptics 收购，而 Synaptics 随后以专利侵权为由要求停止未经授权的实现，并警告可能面临高昂法律处罚。因此，相关扩展坞的 Linux 用户目前似乎仍将无法获得与其他平台相当的功能。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Legal' style='--c:#64748b'>Legal</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/10/top_stories_11102026'><span class='en'>Nov 10</span><span class='zh'>11月10日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>481</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50443824' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>267</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50372929" data-id="50372929" data-tags="AI|Security" data-score="478" data-comments="356" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50372929" data-time="1793835194">
<a class="thumb" href="https://openai.com/index/a-thousand-fewer-paths-to-root/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/400PQ4WTCOvXwwvk1OMOHv/03970ddeafd22fdbc9832669deacc59c/a-thousand-fewer-paths-to-root-social.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='openai.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">50</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://openai.com/index/a-thousand-fewer-paths-to-root/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A thousand fewer paths to root</span><span class='zh'>少一千条通往 root 的路径</span></a><span class="dom">openai.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI built an autonomous pipeline that discovered, validated, and generated patches for 948 local privilege-escalation vulnerabilities in the Linux kernel.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 构建了一个自主流水线，用于发现、验证并为 Linux 内核中的 948 个本地提权漏洞生成补丁。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenAI built an autonomous pipeline that discovered, validated, and generated patches for 948 local privilege-escalation vulnerabilities in the Linux kernel. In a single run using its cyber model, the system validated the vulnerabilities and produced patches at an API cost of $1.2 million. The work suggests that some security investigations can increasingly be handled as a systematic automated process.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 构建了一个自主流水线，用于发现、验证并为 Linux 内核中的 948 个本地提权漏洞生成补丁。在一次运行中，该系统借助其网络安全模型完成了漏洞验证和补丁生成，API 成本为 120 万美元。这项工作表明，部分安全研究正逐渐可以作为系统化的自动流程来执行。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/04/top_stories_11042026'><span class='en'>Nov 4</span><span class='zh'>11月4日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>478</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50372929' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>356</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50368481" data-id="50368481" data-tags="Programming" data-score="465" data-comments="62" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50368481" data-time="1793811907">
<a class="thumb" href="https://declarative-dragging.github.io/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://declarative-dragging.github.io/link-preview.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='declarative-dragging.github.io' data-c='#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://declarative-dragging.github.io/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Declarative Dragging</span><span class='zh'>声明式拖拽</span></a><span class="dom">declarative-dragging.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Declarative dragging proposes that interface authors define which future model states an element may reach by dragging.</span><span class='zh'>“声明式拖拽”提出，界面作者可以通过指定元素拖动后允许到达的模型状态来定义拖拽行为。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Declarative dragging proposes that interface authors define which future model states an element may reach by dragging. This makes a diagram element draggable by describing its allowed outcomes rather than implementing dragging behavior directly. The work was named the Best Paper at UIST 2026.</span><span class='zh'>“声明式拖拽”提出，界面作者可以通过指定元素拖动后允许到达的模型状态来定义拖拽行为。这样，创建可拖动的图表元素不必直接实现拖拽逻辑，而是描述其允许的结果。该研究获得了 UIST 2026 最佳论文奖。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/08/top_stories_11082026'><span class='en'>Nov 8</span><span class='zh'>11月8日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>465</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50368481' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>62</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50380810" data-id="50380810" data-tags="AI|Hardware" data-score="458" data-comments="333" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50380810" data-time="1793894494">

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.watched.earth" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=watched.earth&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>watched.earth</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
